===== M0004 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.exitScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 313-334
Documentation:
/**
     * Rename vars for the current scope, and merge any referenced
     * names into the parent scope reference set.
     */
Method code:
313 |     @Override
314 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
315 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
316 |         return;
317 |       }
318 | 
319 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
320 |         Var v = it.next();
321 |         handleScopeVar(v);
322 |       }
323 | 
324 |       // Merge any names that were referenced but not declared in the current
325 |       // scope.
326 |       Set<String> current = referencedNames;
327 |       referencedNames = referenceStack.pop();
328 |       // If there isn't anything left in the stack we will be going into the
329 |       // global scope: don't try to build a set of referenced names for the
330 |       // global scope.
331 |       if (!referenceStack.isEmpty()) {
332 |         referencedNames.addAll(current);
333 |       }
334 |     }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1105-1125
Documentation:

Method code:
1105 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1106 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1107 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1108 |       return s;
1109 |     }
1110 | 
1111 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1112 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1113 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1114 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1115 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1116 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1117 |       // crazy.
1118 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1119 |         sb.append(c);
1120 |       } else {
1121 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     return sb.toString();
1125 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 684-720
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
684 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
685 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  boolean preferEndOfFileBreak,
686 |                                  int lineLengthThreshold,
687 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
688 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
689 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
690 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
691 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
692 | 
693 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
694 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
695 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
696 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
697 |             lineBreak, preferEndOfFileBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
698 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
699 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
700 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
701 |     CodeGenerator cg =
702 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
703 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
704 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
705 | 
706 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
707 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
708 |     }
709 | 
710 |     cg.add(root);
711 |     mcp.endFile();
712 | 
713 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
714 | 
715 |     if (createSourceMap) {
716 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     return code;
720 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new variable in a synthetic script. This will prevent
   * subsequent compiler passes from crashing.
   */
Method code:
209 |   private void createSynthesizedExternVar(String varName) {
210 |     Node nameNode = IR.name(varName);
211 | 
212 |     // Mark the variable as constant if it matches the coding convention
213 |     // for constant vars.
214 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): honestly, I'm not sure how much this matters.
215 |     // AFAIK, all people who use the CONST coding convention also
216 |     // compile with undeclaredVars as errors. We have some test
217 |     // cases for this configuration though, and it makes them happier.
218 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isConstant(varName)) {
219 |       nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     getSynthesizedExternsRoot().addChildToBack(
223 |         IR.var(nameNode));
224 |     varsToDeclareInExterns.remove(varName);
225 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
226 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 409-422
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
409 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
410 |       Node ret;
411 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
412 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
413 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
414 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
415 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
416 |       } else {
417 |         ret = transform(n);
418 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
419 |       }
420 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
421 |       return ret;
422 |     }

579 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.SYNTAX_ERROR_TYPE));
580 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.REGEXP_TYPE));
581 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.STRING_OBJECT_TYPE));
582 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.STRING_TYPE));
583 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.VOID_TYPE));
584 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.VOID_TYPE), "Undefined");
585 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.VOID_TYPE), "void");
586 |     register(getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE), "Function");
587 |   }

===== M0042 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0045 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 967-994
Documentation:

Method code:
967 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
968 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
969 | 
970 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
971 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
972 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
973 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
974 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
975 |       }
976 |     }
977 | 
978 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
979 |     char quote;
980 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
981 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
982 |       quote = '\'';
983 |       doublequote = "\"";
984 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
985 |     } else {
986 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
987 |       quote = '\"';
988 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
989 |       singlequote = "\'";
990 |     }
991 | 
992 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
993 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
994 |   }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 640-672
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
640 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
641 |       Preconditions.checkState(
642 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
643 |       Node previous = null;
644 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
645 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
646 |       // are already in the right place.
647 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
648 |         previous = current;
649 |         current = current.getNext();
650 |       }
651 | 
652 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
653 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
654 |       while (current != null) {
655 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
656 |         Node next = current.getNext();
657 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
658 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
660 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
661 | 
662 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
663 |           // previous declarations).
664 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
665 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
666 |         } else {
667 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
668 |           previous = current;
669 |         }
670 |         current = next;
671 |       }
672 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

189 |   @Override
190 |   protected List<PassFactory> getChecks() {

197 |     }

200 |       if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==

202 |         checks.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
203 |       } else if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
204 |           AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
205 |         checks.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
206 |       }
207 |       return checks;
208 |     }
209 | 
210 |     if (options.jqueryPass) {
211 |       checks.add(jqueryAliases.makeOneTimePass());
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     checks.add(checkSideEffects);
215 | 
216 |     if (options.checkSuspiciousCode ||
217 |         options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS) ||

219 |       checks.add(suspiciousCode);

221 | 
222 |     if (options.checkControlStructures
223 |         || options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT))  {
224 |       checks.add(checkControlStructures);
225 |     }
226 | 
227 |     if (options.checkRequires.isOn()) {
228 |       checks.add(checkRequires);
229 |     }
230 | 
231 |     if (options.checkProvides.isOn()) {
232 |       checks.add(checkProvides);
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // The following passes are more like "preprocessor" passes.
236 |     // It's important that they run before most checking passes.
237 |     // Perhaps this method should be renamed?
238 |     if (options.generateExports) {
239 |       checks.add(generateExports);
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     if (options.exportTestFunctions) {
243 |       checks.add(exportTestFunctions);
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     if (options.closurePass) {
247 |       checks.add(closurePrimitives.makeOneTimePass());
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     if (options.closurePass && options.checkMissingGetCssNameLevel.isOn()) {
251 |       checks.add(closureCheckGetCssName);
252 |     }
253 | 
254 |     if (options.syntheticBlockStartMarker != null) {
255 |       // This pass must run before the first fold constants pass.
256 |       checks.add(createSyntheticBlocks);
257 |     }
258 | 
259 |     checks.add(checkVars);
260 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
261 |       checks.add(checkRegExp);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.aggressiveVarCheck.isOn()) {
265 |       checks.add(checkVariableReferences);
266 |     }
267 | 
268 |     // This pass should run before types are assigned.
269 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
270 |       checks.add(objectPropertyStringPreprocess);
271 |     }
272 |

274 |       checks.add(resolveTypes.makeOneTimePass());
275 |       checks.add(inferTypes.makeOneTimePass());
276 |       if (options.checkTypes) {
277 |         checks.add(checkTypes.makeOneTimePass());
278 |       } else {
279 |         checks.add(inferJsDocInfo.makeOneTimePass());
280 |       }

285 |       checks.add(checkControlFlow);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // CheckAccessControls only works if check types is on.
289 |     if (options.checkTypes &&

292 |       checks.add(checkAccessControls);

294 |

296 |       checks.add(checkGlobalNames);

300 |       checks.add(checkStrictMode);
301 |     }

304 |     // other checks have been done.
305 |     if (options.closurePass) {
306 |       checks.add(closureReplaceGetCssName);
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     // i18n
310 |     // If you want to customize the compiler to use a different i18n pass,
311 |     // you can create a PassConfig that calls replacePassFactory
312 |     // to replace this.
313 |     checks.add(options.messageBundle != null ?
314 |         replaceMessages : createEmptyPass("replaceMessages"));
315 | 
316 |     if (options.getTweakProcessing().isOn()) {
317 |       checks.add(processTweaks);
318 |     }
319 | 
320 |     // Defines in code always need to be processed.
321 |     checks.add(processDefines);
322 | 
323 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null ||
324 |         options.recordFunctionInformation) {
325 |       checks.add(computeFunctionNames);
326 |     }
327 | 
328 |     if (options.nameReferenceGraphPath != null &&
329 |         !options.nameReferenceGraphPath.isEmpty()) {
330 |       checks.add(printNameReferenceGraph);
331 |     }
332 | 
333 |     if (options.nameReferenceReportPath != null &&
334 |         !options.nameReferenceReportPath.isEmpty()) {
335 |       checks.add(printNameReferenceReport);
336 |     }
337 | 
338 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(checks);
339 |     return checks;
340 |   }

===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1766-1801
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1766 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1767 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1768 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1769 |     }
1770 | 
1771 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1772 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1773 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1774 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1775 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1776 |       skipEOLs();
1777 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1778 |         next();
1779 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1780 |       }
1781 |     }
1782 | 
1783 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1784 | 
1785 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1786 |       next();
1787 |       skipEOLs();
1788 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1789 |       if (memberType != null) {
1790 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1791 | 
1792 |         skipEOLs();
1793 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1794 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1795 |         }
1796 | 
1797 |         next();
1798 |       }
1799 |     }
1800 |     return typeNameNode;
1801 |   }

===== M0140 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 125-146
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
125 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
126 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
127 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
128 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
129 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
130 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
131 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
132 | 
133 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
134 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
135 |         }
136 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
137 |       } else {
138 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
139 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
140 |       }
141 |     }
142 | 
143 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
144 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
145 |     }
146 |   }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }
600 |

602 |     while (it.hasNext()) {

604 |       scopes.push(scope);
605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0171 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1076-1104
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1077 |     // unknown
1078 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1079 |       return true;
1080 |     }
1081 |     // equality
1082 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1083 |       return true;
1084 |     }
1085 |     // all type
1086 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1087 |       return true;
1088 |     }
1089 |     // unions
1090 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1091 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1092 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1093 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1094 |           return true;
1095 |         }
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |     // proxy types
1099 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1100 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1101 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1102 |     }
1103 |     return false;
1104 |   }

/**
     * Determines whether assignment to a define should be allowed

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 120-203
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
122 |     if (!n.isName()) {
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     String varName = n.getString();
127 | 
128 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
129 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
130 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
131 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent));
132 |       return;
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
136 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
137 |     if ((parent.isVar() ||
138 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
139 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
140 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
141 | 
142 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
146 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
147 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
148 |     if (var == null) {
149 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
150 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
151 |         // current scope.
152 |       } else {
153 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
154 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
155 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
156 |         }
157 | 
158 |         if (sanityCheck) {
159 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
160 |         } else {
161 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
162 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
163 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
164 |         }
165 |       }
166 |       return;
167 |     }
168 | 
169 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
170 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
171 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
172 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
173 |       return;
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     // Check module dependencies.
177 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
178 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
179 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
180 |     if (!sanityCheck &&
181 |         varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
182 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
183 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
184 |       } else {
185 |         if (scope.isGlobal()) {
186 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
187 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
188 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
189 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
190 |           } else {
191 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
192 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
193 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
194 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
195 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
196 |           }
197 |         } else {
198 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
199 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     }
203 |   }

===== M0188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0191 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 948-1007
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   private void toString(
949 |       StringBuilder sb,
950 |       boolean printSource,
951 |       boolean printAnnotations,
952 |       boolean printType) {
953 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
954 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getString());
957 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
958 |       sb.append(' ');
959 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
960 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
961 |       // toStringTree.
962 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
963 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
964 |       } else {
965 |         sb.append(first.getString());
966 |       }
967 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
968 |       sb.append(' ');
969 |       sb.append(getDouble());
970 |     }
971 |     if (printSource) {
972 |       int lineno = getLineno();
973 |       if (lineno != -1) {
974 |         sb.append(' ');
975 |         sb.append(lineno);
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printAnnotations) {
980 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
981 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
982 |         int type = keys[i];
983 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
984 |         sb.append(" [");
985 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
986 |         sb.append(": ");
987 |         String value;
988 |         switch (type) {
989 |           default:
990 |             value = x.toString();
991 |             break;
992 |         }
993 |         sb.append(value);
994 |         sb.append(']');
995 |       }
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     if (printType) {
999 |       if (jsType != null) {
1000 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1001 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1002 |           sb.append(" : ");
1003 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1004 |         }
1005 |       }
1006 |     }
1007 |   }

===== M0201 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 910-937
Documentation:

Method code:
910 |     @Override
911 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
912 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
913 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
914 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
915 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
916 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
917 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
918 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
919 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
920 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
921 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
922 |         //
923 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
924 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
925 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
926 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
927 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
928 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
929 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
930 |             (sourceString.substring(
931 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
932 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
933 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       return n;
937 |     }

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CreateSyntheticBlocks.Callback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CreateSyntheticBlocks.java
Original range: 169-215
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |     @Override
170 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
171 |       if (!n.isCall() || !n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
172 |         return;
173 |       }
174 | 
175 |       Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
176 |       String callName = callTarget.getString();
177 | 
178 |       if (startMarkerName.equals(callName)) {
179 |         if (!parent.isExprResult()) {
180 |           compiler.report(
181 |               t.makeError(n, INVALID_MARKER_USAGE, startMarkerName));
182 |           return;
183 |         }
184 |         markerStack.push(parent);
185 |         return;
186 |       }
187 | 
188 |       if (!endMarkerName.equals(callName)) {
189 |         return;
190 |       }
191 | 
192 |       Node endMarkerNode = parent;
193 |       if (!endMarkerNode.isExprResult()) {
194 |         compiler.report(
195 |             t.makeError(n, INVALID_MARKER_USAGE, endMarkerName));
196 |         return;
197 |       }
198 | 
199 |       if (markerStack.isEmpty()) {
200 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
201 |             startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
202 |         return;
203 |       }
204 | 
205 |       Node startMarkerNode = markerStack.pop();
206 |       if (endMarkerNode.getParent() != startMarkerNode.getParent()) {
207 |         // The end marker isn't in the same block as the start marker.
208 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, UNMATCHED_END_MARKER,
209 |             startMarkerName, endMarkerName));
210 |         return;
211 |       }
212 | 
213 |       // This is a valid marker set add it to the list of markers to process.
214 |       validMarkers.add(new Marker(startMarkerNode, endMarkerNode));
215 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType,Node)

Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
390 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
391 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
392 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
393 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
394 | 
395 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
396 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
397 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
398 | 
399 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
400 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
401 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
402 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
403 |     }
404 | 
405 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
406 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
407 |       if (superClass != null) {
408 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
409 |       }
410 | 
411 |       if (isInterface()) {
412 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
413 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
414 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
415 |           }
416 |         }
417 |       }
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
421 |       clearCachedValues();
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     return true;
425 |   }

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 237-265
Documentation:

Method code:
237 |     CollectDefines(AbstractCompiler compiler, List<Name> listOfDefines) {
238 |       this.compiler = compiler;
239 |       this.allDefines = Maps.newHashMap();
240 | 
241 |       assignableDefines = Maps.newHashMap();
242 |       assignAllowed = new ArrayDeque<Integer>();
243 |       assignAllowed.push(1);
244 | 
245 |       // Create a map of references to defines keyed by node for easy lookup
246 |       allRefInfo = Maps.newHashMap();
247 |       for (Name name : listOfDefines) {
248 |         Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
249 |         if (decl != null) {
250 |           allRefInfo.put(decl.node,
251 |                          new RefInfo(decl, name));
252 |         }
253 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
254 |           if (ref == decl) {
255 |             // Declarations were handled above.
256 |             continue;
257 |           }
258 | 
259 |           // If there's a TWIN def, only put one of the twins in.
260 |           if (ref.getTwin() == null || !ref.getTwin().isSet()) {
261 |             allRefInfo.put(ref.node, new RefInfo(ref, name));
262 |           }
263 |         }
264 |       }
265 |     }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 83-131
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   @Override
84 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
85 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
86 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
87 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
88 |     // so we'll allow it.
89 |     if (n.isEmpty() ||
90 |         n.isComma()) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     if (parent == null) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     // Do not try to remove a block or an expr result. We already handle
99 |     // these cases when we visit the child, and the peephole passes will
100 |     // fix up the tree in more clever ways when these are removed.
101 |     if (n.isExprResult() || n.isBlock()) {
102 |       return;
103 |     }
104 | 
105 |     // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
106 |     // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
107 |     if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
108 |       return;
109 |     }
110 | 
111 |     boolean isResultUsed = NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(n);
112 |     boolean isSimpleOp = NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType());
113 |     if (!isResultUsed &&
114 |         (isSimpleOp || !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler()))) {
115 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
116 |       if (n.isString()) {
117 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
118 |       } else if (isSimpleOp) {
119 |         msg = "The result of the '" + Token.name(n.getType()).toLowerCase() +
120 |             "' operator is not being used.";
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       t.getCompiler().report(
124 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
125 |       // TODO(johnlenz): determine if it is necessary to
126 |       // try to protect side-effect free statements as well.
127 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
128 |         problemNodes.add(n);
129 |       }
130 |     }
131 |   }
