===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-951
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case CONSTANT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
258 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
259 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
260 |                   }
261 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
262 |                   continue retry;
263 | 
264 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
265 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
266 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
267 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
268 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
269 |                     } else {
270 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
271 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                     }
273 |                   }
274 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
275 |                   continue retry;
276 | 
277 |                 case DEPRECATED:
278 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
279 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
280 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
281 |                   }
282 | 
283 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
284 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
285 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
286 | 
287 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
288 | 
289 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
290 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
294 |                   continue retry;
295 | 
296 |                 case INTERFACE:
297 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
298 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
299 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
300 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
301 |                     } else {
302 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
303 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
304 |                     }
305 |                   }
306 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case DESC:
310 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
311 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
312 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
313 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
314 |                     continue retry;
315 |                   } else {
316 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
317 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
318 | 
319 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
320 | 
321 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
322 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
323 |                     continue retry;
324 |                   }
325 | 
326 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
327 |                   String fileOverview = "";
328 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
329 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
330 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
331 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
332 | 
333 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
334 | 
335 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
336 |                   } else {
337 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
338 |                   }
339 | 
340 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview)) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
372 |                   }
373 | 
374 |                   if (type == null) {
375 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
376 |                   }
377 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
378 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
379 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
380 |                   }
381 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
382 |                   continue retry;
383 | 
384 |                 case EXPORT:
385 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
386 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
387 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
388 |                   }
389 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
390 |                   continue retry;
391 | 
392 |                 case EXPOSE:
393 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExpose()) {
394 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.expose",
395 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
396 |                   }
397 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
398 |                   continue retry;
399 | 
400 |                 case EXTERNS:
401 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
402 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
403 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
404 |                   }
405 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
406 |                   continue retry;
407 | 
408 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
409 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
410 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
411 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
412 |                   }
413 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
414 |                   continue retry;
415 | 
416 |                 case EXTENDS:
417 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();
419 |                   token = next();
420 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
421 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
422 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
423 | 
424 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
425 |                     token = next();
426 |                     matchingRc = true;
427 |                   }
428 | 
429 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
430 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
431 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
432 | 
433 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
434 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
435 | 
436 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
437 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
438 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
439 |                     }
440 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
441 | 
442 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
443 |                       // record the extended type, check later
444 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
445 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
446 |                     } else {
447 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
448 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
449 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
450 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
451 |                             lineno, charno);
452 |                       }
453 |                     }
454 |                     token = next();
455 |                     if (matchingRc) {
456 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
457 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
458 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
459 |                       }
460 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
461 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
462 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
463 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
464 |                     }
465 |                   } else {
466 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
467 |                   }
468 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
469 |                   continue retry;
470 | 
471 |                 case HIDDEN:
472 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
473 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
474 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
475 |                   }
476 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
477 |                   continue retry;
478 | 
479 |                 case LENDS:
480 |                   skipEOLs();
481 | 
482 |                   matchingRc = false;
483 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
484 |                     token = next();
485 |                     matchingRc = true;
486 |                   }
487 | 
488 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
489 |                     token = next();
490 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
491 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
492 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
493 |                     }
494 |                   } else {
495 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
496 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
497 |                   }
498 | 
499 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
500 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
501 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
502 |                   }
503 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
504 |                   continue retry;
505 | 
506 |                 case MEANING:
507 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
508 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
509 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
510 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
511 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
512 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
513 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
514 |                   }
515 |                   continue retry;
516 | 
517 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
518 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
519 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
520 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
521 |                   }
522 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
523 |                   continue retry;
524 | 
525 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
526 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
527 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
528 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
529 |                   }
530 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
531 |                   continue retry;
532 | 
533 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
534 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
535 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
536 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
537 |                   }
538 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
539 |                   continue retry;
540 | 
541 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
542 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
543 |                   continue retry;
544 | 
545 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
546 |                 case OVERRIDE:
547 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
548 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
549 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
550 |                   }
551 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
552 |                   continue retry;
553 | 
554 |                 case THROWS:
555 |                   skipEOLs();
556 |                   token = next();
557 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
558 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
559 |                   type = null;
560 | 
561 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
562 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
563 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
564 | 
565 |                     if (type == null) {
566 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
567 |                       // recovering parsing
568 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
569 |                       continue retry;
570 |                     }
571 |                   }
572 | 
573 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
574 |                   token = current();
575 | 
576 |                   // Save the throw type.
577 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
578 | 
579 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
580 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
581 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
582 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
583 | 
584 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
585 | 
586 |                     if (description.length() > 0) {
587 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
591 |                   } else {
592 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
593 |                   }
594 |                   continue retry;
595 | 
596 |                 case PARAM:
597 |                   skipEOLs();
598 |                   token = next();
599 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
600 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
601 |                   type = null;
602 | 
603 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
604 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
605 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
606 | 
607 |                     if (type == null) {
608 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
609 |                       // recovering parsing
610 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
611 |                       continue retry;
612 |                     }
613 |                     skipEOLs();
614 |                     token = next();
615 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
616 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
617 |                   }
618 | 
619 |                   String name = null;
620 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
621 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
622 |                     token = next();
623 |                   }
624 | 
625 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
626 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
627 |                         lineno, charno);
628 |                   } else {
629 |                     name = stream.getString();
630 | 
631 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
632 |                       token = next();
633 | 
634 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter
635 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
636 |                       // system.
637 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
638 |                         token = next();
639 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
640 |                           token = next();
641 |                         }
642 |                       }
643 | 
644 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
645 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
646 |                       } else if (type != null) {
647 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
648 |                         // already.
649 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
650 |                       }
651 |                     }
652 | 
653 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
654 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
655 |                     // for handling properties of params.
656 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
657 |                       name = null;
658 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
659 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
660 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
661 |                             lineno, charno);
662 |                       } else {
663 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
664 |                             lineno, charno);
665 |                       }
666 |                     }
667 |                   }
668 | 
669 |                   if (name == null) {
670 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
671 |                     continue retry;
672 |                   }
673 | 
674 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, sourceFile, lineno, charno);
675 | 
676 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
677 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
678 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
679 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
680 | 
681 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
682 | 
683 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
684 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
685 |                           paramDescription);
686 |                     }
687 | 
688 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
689 |                   } else {
690 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
691 |                   }
692 |                   continue retry;
693 | 
694 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
695 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
696 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
697 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
698 |                   }
699 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
700 |                   continue retry;
701 | 
702 |                 case PRIVATE:
703 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
704 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
705 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
706 |                   }
707 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
708 |                   continue retry;
709 | 
710 |                 case PROTECTED:
711 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
712 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
713 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
714 |                   }
715 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
716 |                   continue retry;
717 | 
718 |                 case PUBLIC:
719 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
720 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
721 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
722 |                   }
723 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
724 |                   continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
727 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
728 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
729 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
730 |                   }
731 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
732 |                   continue retry;
733 | 
734 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
735 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
736 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
737 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
738 |                   }
739 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
740 |                   continue retry;
741 | 
742 |                 case MODIFIES:
743 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
747 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
748 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
749 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
750 |                   }
751 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
752 |                   continue retry;
753 | 
754 |                 case SEE:
755 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
756 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
757 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
758 | 
759 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
760 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
761 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
762 |                     } else {
763 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
764 |                     }
765 | 
766 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
767 |                   } else {
768 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
769 |                   }
770 |                   continue retry;
771 | 
772 |                 case SUPPRESS:
773 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
774 |                   continue retry;
775 | 
776 |                 case TEMPLATE:
777 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
778 |                   List<String> names = Lists.newArrayList(
779 |                       Splitter.on(',')
780 |                           .trimResults()
781 |                           .split(templateInfo.string));
782 | 
783 |                   if (names.size() == 0 || names.get(0).length() == 0) {
784 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
785 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
786 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeNames(names)) {
787 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
788 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
789 |                   }
790 | 
791 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
792 |                   continue retry;
793 | 
794 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
795 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
796 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
797 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
798 |                   }
799 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
800 |                   continue retry;
801 | 
802 |                 case VERSION:
803 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
804 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
805 | 
806 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
807 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
808 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
809 |                   } else {
810 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
811 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
812 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
813 |                     }
814 |                   }
815 | 
816 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
817 |                   continue retry;
818 | 
819 |                 case DEFINE:
820 |                 case RETURN:
821 |                 case THIS:
822 |                 case TYPE:
823 |                 case TYPEDEF:
824 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
825 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
826 | 
827 |                   Node typeNode = null;
828 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
829 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
830 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
831 |                     // it as the unknown type.
832 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
833 |                   } else {
834 |                     skipEOLs();
835 |                     token = next();
836 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
837 |                   }
838 | 
839 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
840 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
841 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
842 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
843 |                     }
844 |                   }
845 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
846 | 
847 |                   if (type == null) {
848 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
849 |                     // recovering parsing
850 |                   } else {
851 |                     switch (annotation) {
852 |                       case DEFINE:
853 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
854 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
855 |                               lineno, charno);
856 |                         }
857 |                         break;
858 | 
859 |                       case RETURN:
860 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
861 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
862 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
863 |                           break;
864 |                         }
865 | 
866 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
867 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
868 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
869 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
870 | 
871 |                           String returnDescription =
872 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
873 | 
874 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
875 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
876 |                                 returnDescription);
877 |                           }
878 | 
879 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
880 |                         } else {
881 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
882 |                         }
883 |                         continue retry;
884 | 
885 |                       case THIS:
886 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
887 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
888 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
889 |                         }
890 |                         break;
891 | 
892 |                       case TYPE:
893 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
894 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
895 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
896 |                         }
897 |                         break;
898 | 
899 |                       case TYPEDEF:
900 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
901 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
902 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
903 |                         }
904 |                         break;
905 |                     }
906 |                   }
907 | 
908 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
909 |                   continue retry;
910 |               }
911 |             }
912 |           }
913 |           break;
914 | 
915 |         case EOC:
916 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
917 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
918 |           }
919 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
920 |           return true;
921 | 
922 |         case EOF:
923 |           // discard any accumulated information
924 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
925 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
926 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
927 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
928 |           return false;
929 | 
930 |         case EOL:
931 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
933 |           }
934 |           token = next();
935 |           continue retry;
936 | 
937 |         default:
938 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
939 |             token = next();
940 |             continue retry;
941 |           } else {
942 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
943 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
944 |             continue retry;
945 |           }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1910 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1911 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1912 |   }

===== M0302 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.toObjectType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 606-608
Documentation:
/**
   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.
   *
   * Does not change the underlying JS type. If you want to simulate JS
   * autoboxing or dereferencing, you should use autoboxesTo() or dereference().
   * Those methods may change the underlying JS type.
   */
Method code:
606 |   public ObjectType toObjectType() {
607 |     return this instanceof ObjectType ? (ObjectType) this : null;
608 |   }

===== M0303 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

238 |       if (n.isName()) {
239 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 | 
243 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
245 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
246 |         if (var != null) {
247 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
248 |         }
249 | 
250 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
251 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
252 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
253 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
254 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
255 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
256 |           if (assertOnChange) {
257 |             String name = n.getString();
258 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
259 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
260 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
261 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
262 |           }
263 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
264 |         }
265 |       }
266 |     }

759 |   public void check() {
760 |     runCustomPasses(CustomPassExecutionTime.BEFORE_CHECKS);
761 | 
762 |     // We are currently only interested in check-passes for progress reporting
763 |     // as it is used for IDEs, that's why the maximum progress is set to 1.0.
764 |     PhaseOptimizer phaseOptimizer = new PhaseOptimizer(this, tracker,
765 |         new PhaseOptimizer.ProgressRange(getProgress(), 1.0));
766 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.EVERY_PASS) {
767 |       phaseOptimizer.setSanityCheck(sanityCheck);
768 |     }
769 |     phaseOptimizer.consume(getPassConfig().getChecks());
770 |     phaseOptimizer.process(externsRoot, jsRoot);
771 |     if (hasErrors()) {
772 |       return;
773 |     }
774 | 
775 |     // TODO(nicksantos): clean this up. The flow here is too hard to follow.
776 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
777 |       return;
778 |     }
779 | 
780 |     if (options.removeTryCatchFinally) {
781 |       removeTryCatchFinally();
782 |     }
783 | 
784 |     if (options.getTweakProcessing().shouldStrip() ||
785 |         !options.stripTypes.isEmpty() ||
786 |         !options.stripNameSuffixes.isEmpty() ||
787 |         !options.stripTypePrefixes.isEmpty() ||
788 |         !options.stripNamePrefixes.isEmpty()) {
789 |       stripCode(options.stripTypes, options.stripNameSuffixes,
790 |           options.stripTypePrefixes, options.stripNamePrefixes);
791 |     }
792 |

794 |   }

Original range: 104-147

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

===== M0360 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1051-1053
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1051 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1052 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1053 |   }

464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {

468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }

===== M0389 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 172-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
172 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
173 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
174 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
175 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
176 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
177 |       Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
178 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
179 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
180 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
181 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
182 |           allDefines.add(name);
183 |         } else {
184 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
185 |               decl.getSourceName(),
186 |               decl.node, INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
187 |           compiler.report(error);
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
191 |           if (ref == decl) {
192 |             // Declarations were handled above.
193 |             continue;
194 |           }
195 | 
196 |           Node n = ref.node;
197 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
198 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
199 |           if (info == null &&
200 |               parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
201 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
202 |           }
203 | 
204 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
205 |             allDefines.add(name);
206 |             break;
207 |           }
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
213 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
214 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
215 |   }

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 560-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
560 |     private void extractForInitializer(
561 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
562 | 
563 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
564 |         next = c.getNext();
565 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
566 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
567 |         switch (c.getType()) {
568 |           case Token.LABEL:
569 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
570 |             break;
571 |           case Token.FOR:
572 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
573 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
574 |               if (first.isVar()) {
575 |                 // Transform:
576 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
577 |                 // to:
578 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
579 |                 Node newStatement = first;
580 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
581 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
582 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
583 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
584 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
585 |               }
586 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
587 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
588 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
589 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
590 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
591 | 
592 |               Node newStatement;
593 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
594 |               // but are handled differently.
595 |               if (init.isVar()) {
596 |                 newStatement = init;
597 |               } else {
598 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
599 |               }
600 | 
601 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
602 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
603 |             }
604 |             break;
605 |         }
606 |       }
607 |     }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.

Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3110-3149
Documentation:
/**
   * @returns false iff the result of the expression is not consumed.
   */
Method code:
3110 |   static boolean isExpressionResultUsed(Node expr) {
3111 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider sharing some code with trySimpleUnusedResult.
3112 |     Node parent = expr.getParent();
3113 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
3114 |       case Token.BLOCK:
3115 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
3116 |         return false;
3117 |       case Token.HOOK:
3118 |       case Token.AND:
3119 |       case Token.OR:
3120 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3121 |             ? true : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3122 |       case Token.COMMA:
3123 |         Node gramps = parent.getParent();
3124 |         if (gramps.isCall() &&
3125 |             parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
3126 |           // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
3127 |           // call to an eval. See ECMA-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's OK for the first
3128 |           // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
3129 |           // an eval. This we pretend that this is "used".
3130 |           if (expr == parent.getFirstChild() &&
3131 |               parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
3132 |               expr.getNext().isName() &&
3133 |               "eval".equals(expr.getNext().getString())) {
3134 |             return true;
3135 |           }
3136 |         }
3137 | 
3138 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3139 |             ? false : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3140 |       case Token.FOR:
3141 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
3142 |           // Only an expression whose result is in the condition part of the
3143 |           // expression is used.
3144 |           return (parent.getChildAtIndex(1) == expr);
3145 |         }
3146 |         break;
3147 |     }
3148 |     return true;
3149 |   }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 120-159
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
122 |     new NodeTraversal(
123 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
124 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
125 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
126 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
127 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
128 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
129 |     }
130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.

   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator

2131 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2132 |     switch (operator) {
2133 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2134 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2135 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2136 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2137 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2138 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2139 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2140 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2141 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2142 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2143 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2144 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2145 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2146 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2147 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2148 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2149 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2150 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2151 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
2152 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
2153 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2154 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2155 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2156 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2157 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2158 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2159 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2161 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2162 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2163 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2407-2417
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2407 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2408 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2409 |     int len = s.length();
2410 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2411 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2412 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2413 |         return false;
2414 |       }
2415 |     }
2416 |     return true;
2417 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1240-1362
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1240 |   Node parseInputs() {
1241 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1242 | 
1243 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1244 |     // individual file parse trees.
1245 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1246 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1247 |     }
1248 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1249 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1250 |     }
1251 | 
1252 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1253 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1254 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1255 | 
1256 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1257 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1258 | 
1259 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1260 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1261 | 
1262 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1263 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1264 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1268 | 
1269 |     try {
1270 |       // Parse externs sources.
1271 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1272 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1273 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1274 |           return null;
1275 |         }
1276 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1277 |       }
1278 | 
1279 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1280 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1281 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1285 | 
1286 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1287 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1288 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() && options.closurePass) {
1289 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1290 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1291 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1292 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1293 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1294 |           }
1295 |         }
1296 | 
1297 |         try {
1298 |           inputs =
1299 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1300 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1301 |           staleInputs = true;
1302 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1303 |           report(JSError.make(
1304 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1305 | 
1306 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1307 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1308 |             return null;
1309 |           }
1310 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1311 |           report(JSError.make(
1312 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1313 | 
1314 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1315 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1316 |             return null;
1317 |           }
1318 |         }
1319 |       }
1320 | 
1321 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1322 | 
1323 |       if (staleInputs) {
1324 |         repartitionInputs();
1325 |       }
1326 | 
1327 |       // Build the AST.
1328 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1329 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1330 |         if (n == null) {
1331 |           continue;
1332 |         }
1333 | 
1334 |         if (devMode) {
1335 |           runSanityCheck();
1336 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1337 |             return null;
1338 |           }
1339 |         }
1340 | 
1341 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1342 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1343 | 
1344 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1345 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1346 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1347 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1348 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1349 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1350 |         }
1351 | 
1352 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1353 |       }
1354 | 
1355 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1356 |         return null;
1357 |       }
1358 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1359 |     } finally {
1360 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1361 |     }
1362 |   }

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 617-634
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
617 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
618 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
619 |         next = c.getNext();
620 |         if (c.isVar()) {
621 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
622 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
623 |           }
624 | 
625 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
626 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
627 |             c.removeChild(name);
628 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
629 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
630 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
631 |           }
632 |         }
633 |       }
634 |     }

157 |       case Token.FALSE:
158 |       case Token.NULL:
159 |       case Token.THIS:
160 |       case Token.TRUE:
161 |         validateChildless(n);
162 |         return;
163 | 
164 |       // General unary ops
165 |       case Token.DELPROP:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.NEG:
168 |       case Token.NOT:
169 |       case Token.INC:
170 |       case Token.DEC:
171 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
172 |       case Token.VOID:
173 |       case Token.BITNOT:
174 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
175 |         return;
176 | 
177 |       // General binary ops
178 |       case Token.COMMA:
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |       case Token.AND:
181 |       case Token.BITOR:
182 |       case Token.BITXOR:
183 |       case Token.BITAND:
184 |       case Token.EQ:
185 |       case Token.NE:
186 |       case Token.SHEQ:
187 |       case Token.SHNE:
188 |       case Token.LT:
189 |       case Token.GT:
190 |       case Token.LE:
191 |       case Token.GE:
192 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
193 |       case Token.IN:
194 |       case Token.LSH:
195 |       case Token.RSH:
196 |       case Token.URSH:
197 |       case Token.SUB:
198 |       case Token.ADD:
199 |       case Token.MUL:
200 |       case Token.MOD:
201 |       case Token.DIV:
202 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
203 |         return;
204 | 
205 |       // Assignments
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
218 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
219 |         return;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.HOOK:
222 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
223 |         return;
224 | 
225 |       // Node types that require special handling
226 |       case Token.STRING:
227 |         validateString(n);
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.NUMBER:
231 |         validateNumber(n);
232 |         return;
233 | 
234 |       case Token.NAME:
235 |         validateName(n);
236 |         return;
237 | 
238 |       case Token.GETELEM:
239 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
240 |         return;
241 | 
242 |       case Token.GETPROP:
243 |         validateGetProp(n);
244 |         return;
245 | 
246 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
247 |         validateArrayLit(n);
248 |         return;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
251 |         validateObjectLit(n);
252 |         return;
253 | 
254 |       case Token.REGEXP:
255 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
256 |         return;
257 | 
258 |       case Token.CALL:
259 |         validateCall(n);
260 |         return;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEW:
263 |         validateNew(n);
264 |         return;
265 | 
266 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
267 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
268 |         return;
269 | 
270 |       default:
271 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
272 |             + Token.name(n.getType()), n);
273 |     }
274 |   }

===== M0516 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1252 |       } else {
1253 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1254 |       }
1255 |     }
1256 |     return builder.build();
1257 |   }
