===== M0542 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1557
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1526 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1527 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1529 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1530 |           return false;
1531 |         }
1532 |       }
1533 | 
1534 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1535 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1536 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1537 |         return false;
1538 |       }
1539 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1540 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1541 |         return false;
1542 |       }
1543 |     }
1544 | 
1545 |     if (recurse) {
1546 |       Node n, n2;
1547 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1548 |            n != null;
1549 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1550 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1551 |           return false;
1552 |         }
1553 |       }
1554 |     }
1555 | 
1556 |     return true;
1557 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

414 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
415 |       Preconditions.checkState(

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()

Original range: 2347-2363
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2347 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2348 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2349 |     int c;
2350 |     while (true) {
2351 |       c = stream.getChar();
2352 |       if (c == ' ') {
2353 |         continue;
2354 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2355 |         matchedLc = true;
2356 |         break;
2357 |       } else {
2358 |         break;
2359 |       }
2360 |     }
2361 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2362 |     return matchedLc;
2363 |   }

===== M0569 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
458 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
459 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
460 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
461 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
462 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {

468 |         // will get an error down the line.
469 |         inputs.add(input);
470 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
471 |       }
472 |     }
473 |     return inputs;
474 |   }

===== M0578 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1567-1582
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1567 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1568 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1569 |       String name = getString();
1570 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1571 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1572 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1573 |       if (left == null) {
1574 |         return null;
1575 |       }
1576 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1577 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1578 |       return "this";
1579 |     } else {
1580 |       return null;
1581 |     }
1582 |   }

281 |       case '@':

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1686-1727
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1686 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1687 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1688 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1689 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1690 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1691 |       // {?} - right curly
1692 |       // {?=} - equals
1693 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1694 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1695 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1696 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1697 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1698 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1699 |       token = next();
1700 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1701 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1702 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1703 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1704 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1705 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1706 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1707 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1708 |       }
1709 | 
1710 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1711 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1712 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1713 |     } else {
1714 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1715 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1716 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1717 |           next();
1718 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1719 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1720 |           next();
1721 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1722 |         }
1723 |       }
1724 | 
1725 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1726 |     }
1727 |   }

Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0613 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Denormalize.maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Denormalize.java
Original range: 65-127
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse VARs and EXPR_RESULT node into FOR loop initializers where
   * possible.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private void maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node n, Node parent) {
66 |     // Only SCRIPT, BLOCK, and LABELs can have FORs that can be collapsed into.
67 |     // LABELs are not supported here.
68 |     if (parent == null || !NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
69 |       return;
70 |     }
71 | 
72 |     // Is the current node something that can be in a for loop initializer?
73 |     if (!n.isExprResult() && !n.isVar()) {
74 |       return;
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     // Is the next statement a valid FOR?
78 |     Node nextSibling = n.getNext();
79 |     if (nextSibling == null) {
80 |       return;
81 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isForIn(nextSibling)) {
82 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
83 |       Node forVar = forNode.getFirstChild();
84 |       if (forVar.isName()
85 |           && n.isVar() && n.hasOneChild()) {
86 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
87 |         if (!name.hasChildren()
88 |             && forVar.getString().equals(name.getString())) {
89 |           // OK, the names match, and the var declaration does not have an
90 |           // initializer. Move it into the loop.
91 |           parent.removeChild(n);
92 |           forNode.replaceChild(forVar, n);
93 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
94 |         }
95 |       }
96 |     } else if (nextSibling.isFor()
97 |         && nextSibling.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
98 | 
99 |       // Does the current node contain an in operator?  If so, embedding
100 |       // the expression in a for loop can cause some JavaScript parsers (such
101 |       // as the PlayStation 3's browser based on Access's NetFront
102 |       // browser) to fail to parse the code.
103 |       // See bug 1778863 for details.
104 |       if (NodeUtil.containsType(n, Token.IN)) {
105 |         return;
106 |       }
107 | 
108 |       // Move the current node into the FOR loop initializer.
109 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
110 |       Node oldInitializer = forNode.getFirstChild();
111 |       parent.removeChild(n);
112 | 
113 |       Node newInitializer;
114 |       if (n.isVar()) {
115 |         newInitializer = n;
116 |       } else {
117 |         // Extract the expression from EXPR_RESULT node.
118 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
119 |         newInitializer = n.getFirstChild();
120 |         n.removeChild(newInitializer);
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       forNode.replaceChild(oldInitializer, newInitializer);
124 | 
125 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
126 |     }
127 |   }

===== M0621 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getMainOptimizationLoop()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 678-711
Documentation:
/** Creates the passes for the main optimization loop. */
Method code:
678 |   private List<PassFactory> getMainOptimizationLoop() {
679 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
680 |     if (options.inlineGetters) {
681 |       passes.add(inlineSimpleMethods);
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
685 | 
686 |     if (options.inlineFunctions || options.inlineLocalFunctions) {
687 |       passes.add(inlineFunctions);
688 |     }
689 | 
690 |     if (options.inlineProperties) {
691 |       passes.add(inlineProperties);
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     boolean runOptimizeCalls = options.optimizeCalls
695 |         || options.optimizeParameters
696 |         || options.optimizeReturns;
697 | 
698 |     if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
699 |       if (options.deadAssignmentElimination) {
700 |         passes.add(deadAssignmentsElimination);
701 |       }
702 |       if (!runOptimizeCalls) {
703 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
704 |       }
705 |     }
706 |     if (runOptimizeCalls) {
707 |       passes.add(optimizeCallsAndRemoveUnusedVars);
708 |     }
709 |     assertAllLoopablePasses(passes);
710 |     return passes;
711 |   }

76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));

92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }

Documentation:

Method code:
610 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
611 |   static <T> T runCallable(
612 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
613 | 
614 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the JS Compiler overflows the stack when running on some
615 |     // large or complex JS code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
616 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
617 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
618 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
619 | 
620 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
621 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
622 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
623 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
624 |       @Override
625 |       public void run() {
626 |         try {
627 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
628 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
629 |           }
630 |           result[0] = callable.call();
631 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
632 |           exception[0] = e;
633 |         } finally {
634 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
635 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
636 |           }
637 |         }
638 |       }
639 |     };
640 | 
641 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
642 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
643 |       th.start();
644 |       while (true) {
645 |         try {
646 |           th.join();
647 |           break;
648 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
649 |           // ignore
650 |         }
651 |       }
652 |     } else {
653 |       runnable.run();
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
657 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
658 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     return (T) result[0];
662 |   }

===== M0642 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Run all the passes in the optimizer.
   */
Method code:
177 |   @Override
178 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
179 |     double progress = 0.0;
180 |     double progressStep = 0.0;
181 |     if (progressRange != null) {
182 |       progressStep = (progressRange.maxValue - progressRange.initialValue)
183 |           / passes.size();
184 |       progress = progressRange.initialValue;
185 |     }
186 |     for (CompilerPass pass : passes) {
187 |       pass.process(externs, root);
188 |       if (progressRange != null) {
189 |         progress += progressStep;
190 |         compiler.setProgress(progress);
191 |       }
192 |       if (hasHaltingErrors()) {
193 |         return;
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 286-371
Documentation:

Method code:
286 |     @Override
287 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
288 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
289 |       if (refInfo != null) {
290 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
291 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
292 |         String fullName = name.getFullName();
293 |         switch (ref.type) {
294 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
295 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
296 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
297 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
298 |             if (valParent.isAssign() && name.isSimpleName() &&
299 |                 name.getDeclaration() == ref) {
300 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
301 |               // before it's declared
302 |               compiler.report(
303 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
304 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
305 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
306 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
307 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
308 | 
309 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
310 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
311 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
312 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
313 |             }
314 |             break;
315 |           default:
316 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
317 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
318 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
319 |               // or it's an error.
320 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
321 |               if (info != null) {
322 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
323 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
324 |               }
325 |             }
326 |             break;
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
331 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
332 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
333 |         compiler.report(
334 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
338 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
339 |         if (n.isAssign()) {
340 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
341 |           n.removeChild(last);
342 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
343 |         } else {
344 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
345 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
346 |         }
347 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       if (n.isCall()) {
351 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
352 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
353 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
354 |           //
355 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
356 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
357 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
358 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
359 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, especially because
360 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
361 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
362 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
363 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
364 |           }
365 | 
366 |           assignableDefines.clear();
367 |         }
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
371 |     }

===== M0672 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 664-709
Documentation:

Method code:
664 |   private void compileInternal() {
665 |     setProgress(0.0);
666 |     parse();
667 |     // 15 percent of the work is assumed to be for parsing (based on some
668 |     // minimal analysis on big JS projects, of course this depends on options)
669 |     setProgress(0.15);
670 |     if (hasErrors()) {
671 |       return;
672 |     }
673 | 
674 |     if (!precheck()) {
675 |       return;
676 |     }
677 | 
678 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
679 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
680 |       check();
681 |       return;
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
685 |       check();
686 |       if (hasErrors()) {
687 |         return;
688 |       }
689 | 
690 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
691 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
692 |         externExports();
693 |       }
694 | 
695 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
696 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
697 |         optimize();
698 |       }
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
702 |       recordFunctionInformation();
703 |     }
704 | 
705 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
706 |       runSanityCheck();
707 |     }
708 |     setProgress(1.0);
709 |   }

===== M0699 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0705 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 345-371
Documentation:

Method code:
345 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
346 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
347 |     // two cases:
348 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
349 |     // and
350 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
351 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
352 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
353 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
354 |     //
355 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
356 |     //
357 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
358 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
359 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
360 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
361 |     //
362 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
363 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
364 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
365 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
366 | 
367 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
368 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
369 |     }
370 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
371 |   }

===== M0709 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1367-1404
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1367 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1368 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1369 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1370 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1371 |           options.closurePass) {
1372 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1373 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1374 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1375 |           continue;
1376 |         }
1377 |       }
1378 | 
1379 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1380 | 
1381 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1382 |       if (n == null) {
1383 |         continue;
1384 |       }
1385 | 
1386 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1387 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1388 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1389 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1390 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1391 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1392 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1393 | 
1394 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1395 | 
1396 |         externs.add(input);
1397 |         staleInputs = true;
1398 |       }
1399 |     }
1400 | 
1401 |     if (staleInputs) {
1402 |       repartitionInputs();
1403 |     }
1404 |   }

===== M0723 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3011-3037
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3011 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3012 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3013 |     if (info == null) {
3014 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3015 |       if (parent == null) {
3016 |         return null;
3017 |       }
3018 | 
3019 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3020 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3021 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3022 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3023 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3024 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3025 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3026 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3027 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3028 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3029 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3030 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3031 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3032 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3033 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3034 |       }
3035 |     }
3036 |     return info;
3037 |   }

878 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
879 |     int count = 0;
880 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
881 |       count++;
882 |     }
883 | 
884 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
885 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
886 |       count--;

888 |     }
889 | 
890 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
891 |     return keys;
892 |   }

===== M0728 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1016-1103
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape JavaScript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1016 |   private static String strEscape(
1017 |       String s, char quote,
1018 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1019 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1020 |       String backslashEscape,
1021 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1022 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1023 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1024 |     sb.append(quote);
1025 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1026 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1027 |       switch (c) {
1028 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1029 |         case '\u000B':
1030 |           if (useSlashV) {
1031 |             sb.append("\\v");
1032 |           } else {
1033 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1034 |           }
1035 |           break;
1036 |         // From the SingleEscapeCharacter grammar production.
1037 |         case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
1038 |         case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
1039 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1040 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1041 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1042 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1043 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1044 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1045 | 
1046 |         // From LineTerminators (ES5 Section 7.3, Table 3)
1047 |         case '\u2028': sb.append("\\u2028"); break;
1048 |         case '\u2029': sb.append("\\u2029"); break;
1049 | 
1050 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1051 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1052 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1053 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1054 |             sb.append("\\>");
1055 |           } else {
1056 |             sb.append(c);
1057 |           }
1058 |           break;
1059 |         case '<':
1060 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1061 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1062 | 
1063 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1064 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1065 | 
1066 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1067 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1068 |             sb.append("<\\");
1069 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1070 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1071 |             sb.append("<\\");
1072 |           } else {
1073 |             sb.append(c);
1074 |           }
1075 |           break;
1076 |         default:
1077 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1078 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1079 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1080 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1081 |               sb.append(c);
1082 |             } else {
1083 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1084 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1085 |             }
1086 |           } else {
1087 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight Latin characters
1088 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1089 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1090 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1091 |               sb.append(c);
1092 |             } else {
1093 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some JS parsers,
1094 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1095 |               // so we play it safe and Unicode escape them.
1096 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1097 |             }
1098 |           }
1099 |       }
1100 |     }
1101 |     sb.append(quote);
1102 |     return sb.toString();
1103 |   }

===== M0740 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.JSDocInfoBuilder.build(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/JSDocInfoBuilder.java
Original range: 124-135
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1168-1241
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1189 |       case Token.EQ:
1190 |       case Token.NE:
1191 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1192 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1193 |       case Token.LT:
1194 |       case Token.GT:
1195 |       case Token.LE:
1196 |       case Token.GE:
1197 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1204 |       case Token.MUL:
1205 |       case Token.MOD:
1206 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1207 |       case Token.INC:
1208 |       case Token.DEC:
1209 |       case Token.NEW:

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0766 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getReplacementsHelper(Map<String, Object>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 956-974
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map of String->Node from a map of String->Number/String/Boolean.
   */
Method code:
956 |   private static Map<String, Node> getReplacementsHelper(
957 |       Map<String, Object> source) {
958 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
959 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : source.entrySet()) {
960 |       String name = entry.getKey();
961 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
962 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
963 |         map.put(name, NodeUtil.booleanNode(((Boolean) value).booleanValue()));
964 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
965 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Integer) value).intValue()));
966 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
967 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
968 |       } else {
969 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
970 |         map.put(name, IR.string((String) value));
971 |       }
972 |     }
973 |     return map;
974 |   }

===== M0768 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 994-1050
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
994 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
995 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
996 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
997 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
998 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1002 |       return false;
1003 |     }
1004 | 
1005 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1006 | 
1007 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1008 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1009 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1010 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1011 |         return false;
1012 |       }
1013 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1014 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1015 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1016 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1017 |         return false;
1018 |       }
1019 | 
1020 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1021 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 | 
1025 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1026 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1027 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1028 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1029 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1030 |           return false;
1031 |         }
1032 |       }
1033 | 
1034 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1035 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1036 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1039 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1040 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1041 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1042 |           if (param != null &&
1043 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp()))
1044 |           return false;
1045 |         }
1046 |       }
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     return true;
1050 |   }

===== M0788 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReplaceIdGenerators.GatherGenerators.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReplaceIdGenerators.java
Original range: 110-147
Documentation:

Method code:
110 |     @Override
111 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
112 |       JSDocInfo doc = n.getJSDocInfo();
113 |       if (doc == null) {
114 |         return;
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       if (!doc.isConsistentIdGenerator() &&
118 |           !doc.isIdGenerator()) {
119 |         return;
120 |       }
121 | 
122 |       if (doc.isConsistentIdGenerator() && doc.isIdGenerator()) {
123 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_GENERATOR_TYPE));
124 |       }
125 | 
126 |       String name = null;
127 |       if (n.isAssign()) {
128 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
129 |       } else if (n.isVar()) {
130 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |       } else if (n.isFunction()){
132 |         name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
133 |         if (name.isEmpty()) {
134 |           return;
135 |         }
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // TODO(user): Error on function that has both. Or redeclartion
139 |       // on the same function.
140 |       if (doc.isConsistentIdGenerator()) {
141 |         consistNameGenerators.put(name, createNameSupplier());
142 |         consistNameMap.put(name, Maps.<String, String>newLinkedHashMap());
143 |       } else {
144 |         nameGenerators.put(name, createNameSupplier());
145 |       }
146 |       idGeneratorMaps.put(name, Lists.<Replacement>newArrayList());
147 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.Config.buildAnnotationNames(Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/Config.java
Original range: 88-101
Documentation:
/**
   * Create the annotation names from the user-specified
   * annotation whitelist.
   */
Method code:
88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
89 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {
90 |     ImmutableMap.Builder<String, Annotation> annotationBuilder =
91 |         ImmutableMap.builder();
92 |     annotationBuilder.putAll(Annotation.recognizedAnnotations);
93 |     for (String unrecognizedAnnotation : annotationWhitelist) {
94 |       if (!Annotation.recognizedAnnotations.containsKey(
95 |               unrecognizedAnnotation)) {
96 |         annotationBuilder.put(
97 |             unrecognizedAnnotation, Annotation.NOT_IMPLEMENTED);
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |     return annotationBuilder.build();
101 |   }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
1022 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
1023 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1024 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
1025 |       while (true) {
1026 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1027 |           String name = stream.getString();
1028 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
1029 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
1030 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
1031 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1032 |           }
1033 | 
1034 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
1035 |           token = next();
1036 |         } else {
1037 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1038 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1039 |           return token;
1040 |         }
1041 | 
1042 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1043 |           token = next();
1044 |         } else {
1045 |           break;
1046 |         }
1047 |       }
1048 | 
1049 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1050 |         parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1051 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1052 |       } else {
1053 |         token = next();
1054 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
1055 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
1056 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1057 |         }
1058 |       }
1059 |     }
1060 |     return token;
1061 |   }

===== M0840 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 84-110
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a module graph from a list of modules in dependency order.
   */
Method code:
84 |   public JSModuleGraph(List<JSModule> modulesInDepOrder) {
85 |     Preconditions.checkState(
86 |         modulesInDepOrder.size() == Sets.newHashSet(modulesInDepOrder).size(),
87 |         "Found duplicate modules");
88 |     modules = ImmutableList.copyOf(modulesInDepOrder);
89 |     modulesByDepth = Lists.newArrayList();
90 | 
91 |     for (JSModule module : modulesInDepOrder) {
92 |       int depth = 0;
93 |       for (JSModule dep : module.getDependencies()) {
94 |         int depDepth = dep.getDepth();
95 |         if (depDepth < 0) {
96 |           throw new ModuleDependenceException(String.format(
97 |               "Modules not in dependency order: %s preceded %s",
98 |               module.getName(), dep.getName()),
99 |               module, dep);
100 |         }
101 |         depth = Math.max(depth, depDepth + 1);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       module.setDepth(depth);
105 |       if (depth == modulesByDepth.size()) {
106 |         modulesByDepth.add(new ArrayList<JSModule>());
107 |       }
108 |       modulesByDepth.get(depth).add(module);
109 |     }
110 |   }

===== M0866 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-934
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.AND:
797 |       case Token.BLOCK:
798 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
799 |       case Token.HOOK:
800 |       case Token.IF:
801 |       case Token.IN:
802 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
803 |       case Token.NUMBER:
804 |       case Token.OR:
805 |       case Token.THIS:
806 |       case Token.TRUE:
807 |       case Token.FALSE:
808 |       case Token.NULL:
809 |       case Token.STRING:
810 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
811 |       case Token.SWITCH:
812 |       case Token.TRY:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |         break;
815 | 
816 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         return true;
819 | 
820 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
821 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
822 |           return true;
823 |         }
824 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
825 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
826 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
827 |             return true;
828 |           }
829 |         }
830 |         return false;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
833 |       case Token.REGEXP:
834 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
840 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
841 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
842 |           return true;
843 |         }
844 |         break;
845 | 
846 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
847 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
848 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
849 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
850 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
851 | 
852 |       case Token.NEW:
853 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
854 |           return true;
855 |         }
856 | 
857 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
858 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
859 |           // side-effects
860 |           break;
861 |         }
862 |         return true;
863 | 
864 |       case Token.CALL:
865 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
866 |         // side effect property set.
867 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
868 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
869 |           // side-effects
870 |           break;
871 |         }
872 |         return true;
873 | 
874 |       default:
875 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
876 |           break;
877 |         }
878 | 
879 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
880 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
881 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
882 |             return true;
883 |           }
884 | 
885 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
886 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
887 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
888 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
889 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
890 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
891 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
892 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
893 |             return true;
894 |           }
895 | 
896 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
897 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
898 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
899 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
900 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
901 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
902 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
903 |               return false;
904 |             }
905 | 
906 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
907 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
908 |             // other objects.
909 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
910 |             // side-effect.
911 |             while (isGet(current)) {
912 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
913 |             }
914 | 
915 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
916 |           } else {
917 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
918 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
919 |             // preserve existing behavior.
920 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
921 |           }
922 |         }
923 | 
924 |         return true;
925 |     }
926 | 
927 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
928 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
929 |         return true;
930 |       }
931 |     }
932 | 
933 |     return false;
934 |   }

===== M0874 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard.enables(DiagnosticGroup)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 48-59
Documentation:

Method code:
48 |   @Override
49 |   public boolean enables(DiagnosticGroup otherGroup) {
50 |     if (level.isOn()) {
51 |       for (DiagnosticType type : otherGroup.getTypes()) {
52 |         if (group.matches(type)) {
53 |           return true;
54 |         }
55 |       }
56 |     }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1733 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1734 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1735 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1736 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1737 |       skipEOLs();
1738 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1739 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1740 |       skipEOLs();
1741 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1742 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1743 |       skipEOLs();
1744 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1745 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1746 |       String string = stream.getString();
1747 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1748 |         skipEOLs();
1749 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1750 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1751 |         return newStringNode(string);
1752 |       } else {
1753 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1754 |       }
1755 |     }
1756 | 
1757 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1758 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1759 |   }

===== M0895 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 307-435
Documentation:

Method code:
307 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
308 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
309 |         return;
310 |       }
311 | 
312 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
313 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
314 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
315 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
316 |       }
317 | 
318 |       String name;
319 |       boolean isSet = false;
320 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
321 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
322 | 
323 |       switch (n.getType()) {
324 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
325 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
326 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
327 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
328 |           name = null;
329 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
330 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
331 |           }
332 |           if (name == null) return;
333 |           isSet = true;
334 |           switch (n.getType()) {
335 |             case Token.STRING_KEY:
336 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
337 |               break;
338 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
339 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
340 |               break;
341 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
342 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
343 |               break;
344 |             default:
345 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
346 |           }
347 |           break;
348 |         case Token.NAME:
349 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
350 |           if (parent != null) {
351 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
352 |               case Token.VAR:
353 |                 isSet = true;
354 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
355 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
356 |                 break;
357 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
358 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
359 |                   isSet = true;
360 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
361 |                 }
362 |                 break;
363 |               case Token.GETPROP:
364 |                 return;
365 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
366 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
367 |                 if (gramps == null ||
368 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
369 |                 isSet = true;
370 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
371 |                 break;
372 |               case Token.INC:
373 |               case Token.DEC:
374 |                 isSet = true;
375 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
376 |                 break;
377 |               default:
378 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
379 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
380 |                   isSet = true;
381 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
382 |                 }
383 |             }
384 |           }
385 |           name = n.getString();
386 |           break;
387 |         case Token.GETPROP:
388 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
389 |           if (parent != null) {
390 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
391 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
392 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
393 |                   isSet = true;
394 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
395 |                   isPropAssign = true;
396 |                 }
397 |                 break;
398 |               case Token.INC:
399 |               case Token.DEC:
400 |                 isSet = true;
401 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
402 |                 break;
403 |               case Token.GETPROP:
404 |                 return;
405 |               default:
406 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
407 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
408 |                   isSet = true;
409 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
410 |                 }
411 |             }
412 |           }
413 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
414 |           if (name == null) return;
415 |           break;
416 |         default:
417 |           return;
418 |       }
419 | 
420 |       // We are only interested in global names.
421 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
422 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
423 |         return;
424 |       }
425 | 
426 |       if (isSet) {
427 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
428 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
429 |         } else {
430 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
431 |         }
432 |       } else {
433 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
434 |       }
435 |     }

Method code:
123 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
124 |     boolean changed = false;
125 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
126 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
127 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
128 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
129 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
130 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
131 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
132 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
133 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
134 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
135 |         changed = changed ||
136 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
137 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
138 |       }
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     if (changed) {
142 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
147 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
148 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
149 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
150 |     }
151 |   }

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1637 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1638 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1639 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1640 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1641 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1642 |         next();

1644 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1645 |           next();
1646 |         }
1647 |         skipEOLs();
1648 |         token = next();
1649 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1650 |       }

===== M0903 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0923 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getCodeRemovingPasses()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 714-746
Documentation:
/** Creates several passes aimed at removing code. */
Method code:
714 |   private List<PassFactory> getCodeRemovingPasses() {
715 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
716 |     if (options.collapseObjectLiterals && !isInliningForbidden()) {
717 |       passes.add(collapseObjectLiterals);
718 |     }
719 | 
720 |     if (options.inlineVariables || options.inlineLocalVariables) {
721 |       passes.add(inlineVariables);
722 |     } else if (options.inlineConstantVars) {
723 |       passes.add(inlineConstants);
724 |     }
725 | 
726 |     if (options.foldConstants) {
727 |       // These used to be one pass.
728 |       passes.add(minimizeExitPoints);
729 |       passes.add(peepholeOptimizations);
730 |     }
731 | 
732 |     if (options.removeDeadCode) {
733 |       passes.add(removeUnreachableCode);
734 |     }
735 | 
736 |     if (options.removeUnusedPrototypeProperties) {
737 |       passes.add(removeUnusedPrototypeProperties);
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (options.removeUnusedClassProperties && !isInliningForbidden()) {
741 |       passes.add(removeUnusedClassProperties);
742 |     }
743 | 
744 |     assertAllLoopablePasses(passes);
745 |     return passes;
746 |   }
